of the words, phrases or sentences.

Appendix A
Inner bound for the 3-to-1 CQIC using unstructured IID codes

The proofs of Propositions
2
and
3
rely on the characterization of the best known inner bound using unstructured IID codes for the specific class of channels we refer to as a 3-to-1 CQIC. Specifically, as one notices in examples
0
,
1
, and
2
, users 2 and 3 do not see interference, only user 1 sees interference, so this is referred to as 3-to-1 CQIC, which is defined below.

Definition 5
.

A
3-
CQIC
(\rho_{\underline{x}}\in\mathcal{D}\left(\mathcal{H}_{\underline{Y}}\right):\underline{x}\in\mathcal{\underline{X}},\kappa_{j}:j\in[3])
is a 3-to-1 CQIC if (i) for every
\Delta\in\mathcal{P}\left(\mathcal{H}_{Y_{2}}\right)
,
\tr\left(\left(I\otimes\Delta\otimes I\right)\rho_{x_{1},x_{2},x_{3}}\right)=\tr\left(\left(I\otimes\Delta\otimes I\right)\rho_{\tilde{x}_{1},\tilde{x}_{2},\tilde{x}_{3}}\right)
for every
\underline{x},\uline{\tilde{x}}\in\mathcal{\underline{X}}
satisfying
x_{2}=\tilde{x}_{2}
, and (ii) for every
\mu\in\mathcal{P}\left(\mathcal{H}_{Y_{3}}\right)
,
\tr\left(\left(I\otimes I\otimes\mu\right)\rho_{x_{1},x_{2},x_{3}}\right)=\tr\left(\left(I\otimes I\otimes\mu\right)\rho_{\tilde{x}_{1},\tilde{x}_{2},\tilde{x}_{3}}\right)
for every
\underline{x},\uline{\tilde{x}}\in\mathcal{\underline{X}}
satisfying
x_{3}=\tilde{x}_{3}
.

Since users 2 and 3 do not suffer interference, user 1 doesn’t need to split its message, and it suffices only for users 2 and 3 to split their messages, so a Han-Kobayashi
[
1
]
strategy will only employ message splitting via superposition coding for users 2 and 3. The following inner bound can be proven via simultaneous decoding. Since each user only needs to decode into two codebooks, one can use the technique developed in
[
23
, Sec. V.B]
for simultaneously decoding two codebooks.
We use these inner bounds to prove the strict suboptimality of unstructured IID codes in Appendices
B-A
and
B-B
.

Theorem 4
.

A rate-cost triple
(R_{1},R_{2},R_{3},\tau_{1},\tau_{2},\tau_{3})
is achievable if there exists (i) two finite sets
\mathcal{U}_{2},\mathcal{U}_{3}
, and (ii) a PMF
p_{U_{2}U_{3}\underline{X}}=p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}X_{2}}p_{U_{3}X_{3}}
defined on
\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}\times\mathcal{\underline{X}}
such that
\mathbb{E}[\kappa_{j}(X_{j})]\leq\tau_{j}:j\in[3]
and

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2},U_{3})

(44)

\displaystyle R_{j}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{j}X_{j};Y_{j}):j=2,3

(45)

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{j}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{j}X_{1};Y_{1}|{U_{\text{\sout{$j$}}}})+I(X_{j};Y_{j}|U_{j}):j=2,3,\,\text{\sout{$j$}}\neq j

(46)

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{2},U_{3},X_{1};Y_{1})+I(X_{2};Y_{2}|U_{2})+I(X_{3};Y_{3}|U_{3})

(47)

holds, where all the information quantities are computed with respect to the state

\displaystyle\rho^{U_{2}U_{3}\underline{X}\underline{Y}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\sum_{u_{2},u_{3},\underline{x}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})p_{U_{2}X_{2}}(u_{2},x_{2})p_{U_{3}X_{3}}(u_{3},x_{3})\outerproduct{u_2,u_3,\ulinex}{u_2,u_3,\ulinex}\otimes\rho^{Y_{1}Y_{2}Y_{3}}_{x_{1},x_{2},x_{3}}.

Appendix B
Proof of Proposition
2
and
3

B-A

Proof of Proposition
2

Consider the
3-
CQIC defined in Ex.
1
. It can be verified that
\mathcal{C}_{k}=1-h_{b}(\delta_{k})
for
k=2,3
. From Fact
1
, we have
\mathcal{C}_{1}=h_{b}(\frac{1+\sqrt{1-4\tau(1-\tau)\sin(\varphi)^{2}}}{2})
and
C_{1}=h_{b}(\frac{1+\cos(\varphi)}{2})
. We prove that the rate triple
(\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\mathcal{C}_{3})
is not achievable using unstructured IID codes under the condition

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}+\mathcal{C}_{2}+\mathcal{C}_{3}>C_{1},

(48)

We prove this by contradiction. Suppose there exists PMF
p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}X_{2}}p_{U_{3}X_{3}}
on
\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}\times\mathcal{\underline{X}}
such that
(\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\mathcal{C}_{3})
is achievable. From (
47
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}+\mathcal{C}_{2}+\mathcal{C}_{3}\leq I(U_{2},U_{3},X_{1};Y_{1})+I(X_{2};Y_{2}|U_{2})+I(X_{3};Y_{3}|U_{3}).

If
H(X_{2}|U_{2})=H(X_{3}|U_{3})=0
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}+\mathcal{C}_{2}+\mathcal{C}_{3}\leq I(X_{2},X_{3},X_{1};Y_{1})\leq C_{1}

which violates the condition (
48
). Hence, we should have
H(X_{2}|U_{2})>0
or
H(X_{3}|U_{3})>0
. Without loss of generality, we assume
H(X_{2}|U_{2})>0
. Using the independence of
(U_{2},X_{2})
and
(U_{3},X_{3})
, we have

\displaystyle 0<H(X_{2}|U_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H(X_{2}|X_{3},U_{3},U_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H(X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|X_{3},U_{3},U_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle H(X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|U_{3},U_{2}).

Hence,
H(X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|U_{2},U_{3})>0
, which guarantees the existence of
(u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}
such that
H(X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|U_{2}=u_{2}^{*},U_{3}=u_{3}^{*})>0
.

Lemma 2
.

Let
\mathcal{X}=\mathcal{B}=\{0,1\}
,
\mathcal{H}_{Y}=\mathbb{C}^{2}
and
\rho_{x}=\outerproduct{0}{0}\mathds{1}\{x=0\}+\outerproduct{v_{\varphi}}{v_{\varphi}}\mathds{1}\{x=1\}
. Then
I(X;Y)\leq I(X;Y|B)
, where the mutual information
I(.,.)
is with respect to the state

\rho^{XBY}=\sum_{x}\sum_{b}p_{X}(x)p_{B}(b)\outerproduct{x,b}{x,b}\otimes\rho_{x\oplus b}.

Moreover, if
H(Y|B)>0
, and
H(Y|X)>0
, then the inequality is strict, i.e.,
I(X;Y)<I(X;Y|B)
.

Proof.

From Fact
1
, we have

\displaystyle I(X;Y)=h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X}(1)*p_{B}(1)\right)\right)-h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{B}(1)\right)\right),\mbox{ and }I(X;Y|B)=h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X}(1)\right)\right),

where
f(t)=\frac{1+\sqrt{1-4t(1-t)\sin(\varphi)^{2}}}{2}
for
t\in[0,\frac{1}{2}]
.
It is easy to verify that if (1)
H(Y|B)=0
, then
I(X;Y)=I(X;Y|B)=0
and if (2)
H(Y|X)=0
, then
I(X;Y)=I(X;Y|B)=h_{b}\left(f(p_{X}(1))\right)
. Now, if
H(Y|B)>0
and
H(Y|X)>0
, we define
g(t)=h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X}(1)*t\right)\right)-h_{b}\left(f(t)\right)
for
t\in[0,\frac{1}{2}]
. Observe that
g(p_{B}(1))=I(X;Y)
and
g(0)=I(X;Y|B)
.
Therefore, we need to show that
g(p_{B}(1))<g(0)
.

By Taylor series,
g(p_{B}(1))=g(0)+p_{B}(1)g^{\prime}(\tilde{t})
for some
\tilde{t}\in(0,p_{B}(1))
. Hence, it suffices to prove
g^{\prime}(t)<0
for all
t\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.
We abbreviate
p_{X}(1)=p
and we calculate
g^{\prime}(t)
. It can be verified that

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(t)=\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\Big)^{(1-2p)f^{\prime}(t*p)}\right)-\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t)}\right).

Since
\log(x^{a+b})=\log(x^a)+\log(x^b)
, we split the first term into two separate terms. We obtain

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(t)=\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t*p)}\right)+\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\Big)^{-2pf^{\prime}(t*p)}\right)-\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t)}\right).

Note that (i) for
t\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,
0\leq\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\leq 1
and
-2pf^{\prime}(t*p)>0
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\Big)^{-2pf^{\prime}(t*p)}\right)\leq 0.

(ii) Define
\gamma(t)=\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t)}\right)
. Since
f
is convex (
f^{\prime\prime}\geq 0
),
\frac{(f^{\prime}(t))^{2}}{f(t)(1-f(t))}>0
, and
\log(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)})\leq 0
for
t\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\gamma^{\prime}(t)=f^{\prime\prime}(t)\log(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)})-\frac{(f^{\prime}(t))^{2}}{f(t)(1-f(t))}\leq 0.

Therefore,
\gamma(t*p)<\gamma(t)
, which implies

\displaystyle\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t*p)}{f(t*p)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t*p)}\right)<\log\left(\Big(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)}\Big)^{f^{\prime}(t)}\right).

Combining (i) and (ii), we conclude that
g^{\prime}(t)<0
for

t\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.
∎

Next, observe that,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}<I(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2},U_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{u_{2}}\sum_{u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3})

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\sum_{u_{2}}\sum_{u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I(X_{1};Y_{1}|X_{2}\oplus X_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(X_{1};Y_{1}|X_{2}\oplus X_{3},U_{2},U_{3})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}(f(p_{X}(1)))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1},

where the first inequality follows from (i) the existence of
(u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}
such that
H(X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|U_{2}=u_{2}^{*},U_{3}=u_{3}^{*})>0
, which implies
H(Y_{1}|X_{1},U_{2}=u_{2}^{*},U_{3}=u_{3}^{*})>0
and (ii) by substituting
p_{X_{1}}
for
p_{X}
and
p_{X_{2}\oplus X_{3}|U_{2},U_{3}}
for
p_{B}
in lemma
2
. The last inequality follows from the cost constraint on
X_{1}
.
Thus, the rate triple
(\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\mathcal{C}_{3})
is not achievable.

B-B

Proof of Proposition
3

Consider the
3-
CQIC defined in Ex.
2
. It can be verified that
\mathcal{C}_{j}=h_{b}(\tau_{j}*\delta_{j})-h_{b}(\delta_{j})
for
j=2,3
. From Fact
1
, we have
\mathcal{C}_{1}=h_{b}(f(\tau_{1}))
and
C_{1}=h_{b}(f(\tau_{1}*\beta))
, where
\beta=\tau_{2}+\tau_{3}-\tau_{2}\tau_{3}
and
f(t)=\frac{1+\sqrt{1-4t(1-t)\sin(\varphi)^{2}}}{2}
for
t\in[0,\frac{1}{2}]
.

We prove that the rate triple
(\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\mathcal{C}_{3})
is not achievable using unstructured codes under the condition

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}+\mathcal{C}_{2}+\mathcal{C}_{3}<C_{1}.

(49)

We prove this by contradiction. Suppose there exists a PMF
p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}X_{2}}p_{U_{3}X_{3}}
defined on
\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}\times\mathcal{\underline{X}}
such that
(\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\mathcal{C}_{3})
is achievable under the condition (
49
).
We define a characterization of such PMF
p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}X_{2}}p_{U_{3}X_{3}}
and use it to derive a contradiction. Observe that

(i) from (
45
) and for
j=2,3
, we have

\displaystyle R_{j}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I\left(U_{j}X_{j};Y_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(Y_{j}\right)-H\left(Y_{j}|U_{j}X_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(\sum_{x_{j}}p_{X_{j}}(x_{j})\rho_{x_{j}}^{Y_{j}}\right)-\sum_{u_{j}}\sum_{x_{j}}p_{U_{j}X_{j}}(u_{j},x_{j})H\left(\rho_{x_{j}}^{Y_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(p_{X_{j}}(1)*\delta_{j}\right)-h_{b}\left(\delta_{j}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(\tau_{j}*\delta_{j}\right)-h_{b}\left(\delta_{j}\right),

where the last inequality follows from the following observations
0\leq p_{X_{j}}(1)\leq\tau_{j}<\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle p_{X_{j}}(1)*\delta_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{X_{j}}(1)(1-2\delta_{j})+\delta_{j}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau_{j}(1-2\delta_{j})+\delta_{j}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-2\delta_{j})+\delta_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2},

and the fact that
h_{b}(.)
is strictly increasing on
[0,\frac{1}{2}]
, which implies
h_{b}(p_{X_{j}}(1)*\delta_{j})
is strictly increasing in
p_{X_{j}}(1)
. Note that
R_{j}=h_{b}\left(\tau_{j}*\delta_{j}\right)-h_{b}\left(\delta_{j}\right)
if and only if
p_{X_{j}}(1)=\tau_{j}
.

(ii) From (
44
), we have

\displaystyle R_{1}\leq I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2},U_{3}\right)=\sum_{u_{2},u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right),

substituting
p_{X_{1}}
for
p_{X}
and
p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(.|u_{2},u_{3})
for
p_{B}
in lemma
2
, we obtain

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{u_{2},u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|X_{2}\lor X_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{2},u_{3}\\
x_{2}\lor x_{3}\end{subarray}}p_{U_{2}U_{3}X_{2}\lor X_{3}}(u_{2},u_{3},x_{2}\lor x_{3})I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|X_{2}\lor X_{3}=x_{2}\lor x_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u_{2},u_{3}\\
x_{2}\lor x_{3}\end{subarray}}p_{U_{2}U_{3}X_{2}\lor X_{3}}(u_{2},u_{3},x_{2}\lor x_{3})

\displaystyle\left[H\left(Y_{1}|X_{2}\lor X_{3}=x_{2}\lor x_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)-H\left(Y_{1}|X_{1},X_{2}\lor X_{3}=x_{2}\lor x_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}}(x_{2}\lor x_{3})\left[H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}\right)-\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})H\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}\right)\right].

since
H\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}\right)=0
, and
H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}\oplus 1}\right)=H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}}\right)=h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{1}}(1)\right)\right)
, we obtain

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}}(x_{2}\lor x_{3})H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho_{x_{1}}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{1}}(1)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(\tau_{1}\right)\right),

where the last inequality follows from

0\leq p_{X_{1}}(1)\leq\tau_{1}<\frac{1}{2}

and from

h_{b}\left(f\left(t\right)\right)^{\prime}=\frac{(2t-1)\sin^{2}(\varphi)}{\sqrt{1-4t(1-t)\sin^{2}(\varphi)}}\,\log\left(\frac{1-f(t)}{f(t)}\right)>0,\>\>t\in(0,1/2),

which implies that
h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{1}}(1)\right)\right)
is strictly increasing in
p_{X_{1}}(1)
. Moreover,
R_{1}=h_{b}\left(f\left(\tau_{1}\right)\right)
if and only if
p_{X_{1}}(1)=\tau_{1}
.

(iii) Suppose
H(X_{2}|U_{2})=H(X_{3}|U_{3})=0
. Therefore, from (
47
), we have

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I\left(X_{1},X_{2},X_{3};Y_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(Y_{1}\right)-H\left(Y_{1}|X_{1},X_{2},X_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(\sum_{\underline{x}}p_{\underline{X}}(\underline{x})\rho_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}^{Y_{1}}\right)-\sum_{\underline{x}}p_{\underline{X}}(\underline{x})H\left(\rho_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{1}}(1)*p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}}(1)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(\tau_{1}*\beta\right)\right),

where the last inequality follows from substituting
p_{X_{1}}(1)=\tau_{1}
and
p_{X_{j}}(1)=\tau_{j}:j=2,3
as obtained in (i) and (ii). This violates the hypothesis (
49
). Hence, we should have
H(X_{2}|U_{2})>0
or
H(X_{3}|U_{3})>0
.

(iv) Without loss of generality, we assume
H(X_{2}|U_{2})>0
. Therefore, there exists
u_{2}^{*}\in\mathcal{U}_{2}
such that
p_{U_{2}}(u_{2}^{*})>0
and
H(X_{2}|U_{2}=u_{2}^{*})>0
which implies
p_{X_{2}|U_{2}}(x_{2}|u_{2}^{*})>0
for
x_{2}\in\{0,1\}
. Additionally, from (i), we have
0<1-\tau_{3}=p_{X_{3}}(0)=\sum_{u_{3}}p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})p_{X_{3}|U_{3}}(0|u_{3})
which ensures the existence of
u_{3}^{*}\in\mathcal{U}_{3}
such that
p_{U_{3}}(u_{3}^{*})>0
and
0<p_{X_{3}|U_{3}}(0|u_{3}^{*})\leq 1
. Hence, there exists
(u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}
such that

\displaystyle\mbox{ (a) }p_{U_{2}}(u_{2}^{*})>0,\>p_{U_{3}}(u_{3}^{*})>0,\mbox{ (b) }0<p_{X_{2}|U_{2}U_{3}}(x_{2}|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})<1,\mbox{ for }x_{2}\in\{0,1\},\mbox{ and (c) }0<p_{X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(0|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\leq 1,

where (b) and (c) follow from the independence of the pair
(U_{2},X_{2})
and
(U_{3},X_{3})
. Therefore,
0<p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(x|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})<1,\mbox{ for }x\in\{0,1\}
.
Hence

\displaystyle H\left(X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2},U_{3}\right)=\sum_{u_{2},u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})H\left(X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)>0.

(v) From (
44
), we have

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2},U_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{u_{2},u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right).

From (iv), there exists
(u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{2}\times\mathcal{U}_{3}
such that

\displaystyle H\left(Y_{1}|X_{1},U_{2}=u_{2}^{*},U_{3}=u_{3}^{*}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})H\left(\sum_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(x_{2}\lor x_{3}|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\rho_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(\sum_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(x_{2}\lor x_{3}|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\rho_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(1|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})\right)\right)>0.

And since
0<\tau_{1}=p_{X_{1}}(1)
, we have

\displaystyle H\left(Y_{1}|X_{2}\lor X_{3},U_{2}=u_{2}^{*},U_{3}=u_{3}^{*}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{2}\lor x_{3}}p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(x_{2}\lor x_{3}|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho_{x_{1}\oplus(x_{2}\lor x_{3})}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\left(\sum_{x_{1}}p_{X_{1}}(x_{1})\rho_{x_{1}}^{Y_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(p_{X_{1}}(1)\right)\right)>0.

Consequently, by substituting
p_{X_{1}}
for
p_{X}
and
p_{X_{2}\lor X_{3}|U_{2}U_{3}}(.|u_{2}^{*},u_{3}^{*})
for
p_{B}
in lemma
2
, we have

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\sum_{u_{2},u_{3}}p_{U_{2}}(u_{2})p_{U_{3}}(u_{3})I\left(X_{1};Y_{1}|X_{2}\lor X_{3},U_{2}=u_{2},U_{3}=u_{3}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{b}\left(f\left(\tau_{1}\right)\right).

The last equality follows from the same steps derived in (ii). Hence
R_{1}<h_{b}\left(f(\tau_{1})\right)=\mathcal{C}_{1}
. We have thus derived a contradiction
\mathcal{C}_{1}=R_{1}<\mathcal{C}_{1}
.

Appendix C
Proof of Proposition
4

Observe that

\displaystyle t_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\left|\mathds{1}{\left\{a_{2}(m_{2})=a_{2}\right\}}\mathds{1}{\left\{a_{3}(m_{3})=a_{3}\right\}}-\frac{r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}))}{\upsilon^{k_{2}}}\frac{r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}{\upsilon^{k_{3}}}\right|,

\displaystyle\left|\tr\left\{\left(I-\mu^{Y_{1}}_{m_{1},s}\right)\left(\rho_{x_{1}^{n}(m_{1}),f_{2}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2})),f_{3}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\left|\mathds{1}{\left\{a_{2}(m_{2})=a_{2}\right\}}\mathds{1}{\left\{a_{3}(m_{3})=a_{3}\right\}}-\frac{r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}))}{\upsilon^{k_{2}}}\frac{r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}{\upsilon^{k_{3}}}\right|,

We evaluate the expectation over
C_{2}(m_{2})
and
C_{3}(m_{3})
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\sum_{c_{2}(m_{2})}\sum_{c_{3}(m_{3})}P\left(C_{2}(m_{2})=\left(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}):a_{2}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle P\left(C_{3}(m_{3})=\left(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}):a_{3}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{3}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}))}{\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{2},m_{2}))}\frac{r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}{\sum_{\tilde{a}_{3}}r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{3},m_{3}))}-\frac{r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}))}{\upsilon^{k_{2}}}\frac{r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}{\upsilon^{k_{3}}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\sum_{c_{2}(m_{2})}\sum_{c_{3}(m_{3})}P\left(C_{2}(m_{2})=\left(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}):a_{2}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle P\left(C_{3}(m_{3})=\left(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}):a_{3}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{3}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\frac{r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}))}{\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{2},m_{2}))}\frac{r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}))}{\sum_{\tilde{a}_{3}}r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{3},m_{3}))}\left|1-\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}+k_{3}}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{3}}r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{2},m_{2}))r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{3},m_{3}))\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{c_{2}(m_{2})}\sum_{c_{3}(m_{3})}P\left(C_{2}(m_{2})=\left(u_{2}^{n}(a_{2},m_{2}):a_{2}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle P\left(C_{3}(m_{3})=\left(u_{3}^{n}(a_{3},m_{3}):a_{3}\in\mathcal{F}_{\upsilon}^{k_{3}}\right)\right)\left|\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}+k_{3}}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{3}}r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{2},m_{2}))r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{3},m_{3}))-1\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}+k_{3}}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{3}}r_{U_{2}}^{n}(U_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{2},M_{2}))r_{U_{3}}^{n}(U_{3}^{n}(\tilde{a}_{3},M_{3}))-1\right|\right],

Using the ‘cloud mixing’ lemma
[
35
, Lemma 19]
, we have
\mathbb{E}[t_{1}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\frac{k_{j}}{n}\log(\Prime)>\log(\Prime)-H(U_{j}),\mbox{ for }j=2,3.

Appendix D
Proof of Proposition
5
: Closeness of the states

We mimic the steps in
[
5
]
in this proof.
For any
\ket{h}\in\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ketbra{h})-\ketbra{h}}_{1}\leq 2\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(\bra{h})-\bra{h}\right)\left(\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>-<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),h>-<h,\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>+<h,h>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2-\frac{2}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}},

where the last equality follows from the fact that
\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}
is an isometry and

\displaystyle<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),h>=<h,\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}}.

Using this, we obtain

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ketbra{h})-\ketbra{h}}_{1}\leq 2\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}\leq 2\sqrt{2-2e^{-2\eta^{2}}}\leq 4\eta,

where the first inequality follows from
\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}}>e^{-2\eta^{2}}
and the last inequality follows from using
e^{-x}\geq 1-x
.

Appendix E
Proof of Proposition
6

We first prove a property of the decoding POVM elements
G_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}^{\mathcal{J}}
, for
\mathcal{J}\in\{1,2,3\}
, that will be applied in this appendix.
For
\mathcal{J}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{G^{1}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{(x_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\>\Pi^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\Pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{(x_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\Pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\>\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\Pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{(x_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{(x_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\norm{\Pi_{u^n}^{Y_1} \: \rho^{Y_1}_{x_1^n,u^n} \: \Pi_{u^n}^{Y_1}-\rho^{Y_1}_{x_1^n,u^n}}_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon},

The first equality follows from the definition of
G^{1}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
in (
7
). The second equality follows from the cyclicity of the trace. The first inequality follows from the trace inequality
\tr\left(\Lambda\rho\right)\geq\tr\left(\Lambda\sigma\right)-\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, for
0\leq\Lambda,\rho,\sigma\leq I
. The last inequality follows from (i) the Gentle Operator Lemma
[
31
]
and (ii) from the typicality property

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\mbox{ for }(x_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{X_{1}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}).

The same argument applies for
\mathcal{J}\in\{2,3\}
.

Now, we evaluate the expectation of
t_{2.1.1}
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{2.1.1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I-\gamma^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}-\tr\left\{\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}+\beta^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\beta^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+\norm{\theta_{x_1^n,d^n_1,u^n,d^n_2}- \left(\rho^{Y_1}_{x_1^n,u^n}\otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{1}\right]

where (a) follows from (i) the definition
\gamma^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}
in (
10
), (ii) the cyclicity of the trace and (iii) the property of projectors, (b) follows from applying the non-commutative union bound
[
5
, Fact.3]
, and (c) follows from (i) substituting
\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}
with
\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)
and (ii) because
\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\right)\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}=0
, since
\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)
lives in
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
and
I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}
is a projector onto the complement of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
.

Now applying Prop.
5
and
[
5
, Corollary 1]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{2.1.1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{6}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{1}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{2}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{3}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{6}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\left[1-\tr\left\{G^{1}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}+1-\tr\left\{G^{2}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}+1-\tr\left\{G^{3}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{72}{\eta^{2}}(\epsilon+2\sqrt{\epsilon}),

where (a) follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}_{x_{1}^{n},u^{n}}^{\mathcal{J}}
, for
\mathcal{J}\in\{1,2,3\}
in (
8
) and (ii) from Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
34
, Thm. 3.7]
, and (b) follows from the property stated at the beginning of this appendix, for all
\mathcal{J}\in\{1,2,3\}
.
Chossing
\eta=\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{2.1.1}]\leq 16\eta^{\frac{1}{5}}+72\epsilon^{\frac{3}{5}}+144\epsilon^{\frac{1}{10}}.

Appendix F
Proof of Proposition
7

Analysis of
t_{2.1.2}

We evaluate the expectation of
t_{2.1.2}
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{2.1.2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{2n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},x_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})

(50)

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\theta_{u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\},

where the first equality follows from

\displaystyle\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})\theta_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}},

and the first inequality is obtained from

\displaystyle\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1+\eta^{2}}{1+2\eta^{2}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),\mbox{ where }

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}

\displaystyle\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}-\frac{1+\eta^{2}}{1+2\eta^{2}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
5
]
. We upper-bound the first term in (
50
) using

\displaystyle\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\leq\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\leq\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right),

we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\Pi_{\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}\right)}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(u^{n})

\displaystyle 2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}\>\frac{3\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}}\>2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}||q_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})-\tilde{\delta}-\frac{(k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}})}{n}\log(\Prime)\right)},

where (a) follows because
\gamma^{*}_{x_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}=0
, if
u^{n}\notin T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})
,
(b) follows from the inequality in (

), (c) follows from the trace inequality in (

) and (d) follows from (

) and from

as stated in
[
5
]
. Hence, if we choose

, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{2.1.3}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}||q_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})-\tilde{\delta}-\frac{(k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}})}{n}\log(\Prime)\right)}\left(2^{-n\left(I(U_{2}\oplus U_{3};Y_{1}|X_{1})-2\delta\right)}+2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}\>\frac{3\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\>2^{-n\left(I(U_{2}\oplus U_{3};Y_{1}|X_{1})+D(p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}||q_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})-\tilde{\delta}-2\delta-\frac{(k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}})}{n}\log(\Prime)\right)}.

if

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{2}\oplus U_{3};Y_{1}|X_{1})+D(p_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}||q_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(U_{2}\oplus U_{3};Y_{1}|X_{1})+\log(\Prime)-H(U_{2}\oplus U_{3}).

where (a) follows because (i)

, (d) follows from

) and from (ii) the cyclicity of the trace, and finally (e) follows from the typical projetor properties

\displaystyle\Pi^{Y_{1}}\>\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}\right)^{\otimes n}\>\Pi^{Y_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(H(Y_{1})-\delta)}\>\Pi^{Y_{1}},\mbox{ and }

), the property defined in (

) and

]

we upper-bound the second term in (

), as follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{\mathsf{b}}+l_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{s,\tilde{s}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}p_{X_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{x}_{1}^{n})q_{U_{2}\oplus U_{3}}^{n}(\tilde{u}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{x}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},\tilde{u}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\left(\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}\right)^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

Proof of Proposition

We evaluate the expectation of
t_{2.1}
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[t_{2.1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}+k_{3}}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u_{3}^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})q_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right)\rho_{x_{1}^{n},f_{2}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}),f_{3}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})}^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}+k_{3}}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{3}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u_{3}^{n}}\sum_{x_{1}^{n}}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})p_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})p_{X_{1}}^{n}(x_{1}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right)\rho_{x_{1}^{n},f_{2}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}),f_{3}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})}^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{2}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right)\rho_{u_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{2}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{k_{2}}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\left[\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right)\rho_{u_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\right\}+\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\notin T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})\right],

where (a) follows from using
q_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})=p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})
and
q_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})=p_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})
, and (b) follows from the definition of
\rho_{u_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}
in (
11
).
Observe that,

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\right\}-\norm{\Pi^{Y_2} \rho^{Y_2}_{u_2^n} \Pi^{Y_2} - \rho^{Y_2}_{u_2^n}}_{1}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon},

where the first equality follows from the cyclicity of the trace, the first inequality follows from using
\tr\left(\Lambda\rho\right)\geq\tr\left(\Lambda\sigma\right)-\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, for
0\leq\Lambda,\rho,\sigma\leq I
and the second inequality follows from (i) the Gentle Operator Lemma
[
31
]
and from (ii) the conditional typical property

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\mbox{ for }u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}}).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{2.1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\left(1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon}\right)\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\epsilon+2\sqrt{\epsilon}.

\displaystyle r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\rho_{x_{1}^{n},f_{2}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}),f_{3}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})}^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{2}\neq m_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right\}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2})-\delta\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})2^{-n\left(I(Y_{2};U_{2})-2\delta\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(I(U_{2};Y_{2})+D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-2\delta-\delta_{2}-\frac{k_{2}+l_{2}}{n}\log(\Prime)\right)},

where (a) follows from
q_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})r_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})=p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})
and
q_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})r_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})=p_{U_{3}}^{n}(u_{3}^{n})
, (b) follows from the definition of
\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}
in (
12
), (c) follows from (i) the cyclicity of the trace and (ii) from

\displaystyle q_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n})\leq 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}),\mbox{ for }u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}}),

(d) and (e) follow from the typicality properties, i.e., for

u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{Y_{2}}\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\Pi^{Y_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\Pi^{Y_{2}},\mbox{ and }

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\Pi^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2})+\delta\right)}.

Hence, for
n
sufficiently large, we have
\mathbb{E}[t_{2.3}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\frac{k_{2}+l_{2}}{n}\log(\Prime)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{2};Y_{2})+D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(U_{2};Y_{2})+\log(\Prime)-H(U_{2}).

Following the same steps as above, for
n
sufficiently large, we have
\mathbb{E}[t_{2.2}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\frac{k_{2}}{n}\log(\Prime)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(U_{2};Y_{2})+D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(U_{2};Y_{2})+\log(\Prime)-H(U_{2}).

Appendix I
Proof of Proposition
14

We evaluate the expectation of
t_{1.1}
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\gamma^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\left[\tr\left\{\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)-\tr\left\{\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}\right\}\right],

where the last equality follows from (i) the definition of
\gamma^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}
in (
34
), (ii) the cyclicity of the trace and (iii) the property of the projector i.e.,
\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}^{2}=\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}
. Subsequently, we apply the non-commutative union bound
[
5
, Fact.3]
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}+\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(56)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}+\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\norm{\theta_{\ulinez^n, \ulined^n}-\left(\xi_{\ulinez^n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{1},

where the last inequality follows from the trace inequality
\tr(\Lambda\rho)\leq\tr(\Lambda\sigma)+\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, with
0\leq\Lambda,\rho,\sigma\leq I
.
From Prop.
13
, we bound the second term in (
56
) by
12\eta
and since the state
\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)
lives in
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}
and
I-\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}
is a projector onto its complement, we are left with

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+12\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{42}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\left[\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}\right]+12\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{42}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\left[15-\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}\right]+12\eta,

where the second inequality follows from
[
5
, Corollary.1]
and the equality follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}_{\underline{z}^{n}}^{S}
in (
32
) and (ii) from Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
34
, Thm. 3.7]
. For
S\subseteq[4]
, observe that,

\displaystyle\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\Pi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}

\displaystyle\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}-\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\norm{ \Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}} \xi_{\ulinez^n} \Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}} - \xi_{\ulinez^n}}_{1}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}

\displaystyle 1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon},

where (a) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (b) follows from the trace inequality
\tr\left(\Lambda\rho\right)\geq\tr\left(\Lambda\sigma\right)-\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, for
0\leq\Lambda,\rho,\sigma\leq I
, and (c) follows from (i) the Gentle Operator Lemma
[
31
]
and (ii) from

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\mbox{ for }\underline{z}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}}).

Hence, using the above inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.1}]\leq\frac{168}{\eta^{2}}(15\epsilon+30\sqrt{\epsilon})+12\eta.

With
\eta=\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.1}]\leq 168(15\epsilon^{\frac{3}{5}}+30\epsilon^{\frac{1}{10}})+12\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}.

Appendix J
Proof of Proposition
15

We elaborate on the analysis of
\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}t_{1S}
, and in doing so we leverage the
smoothing
and
augmentation

[
5
]
properties of the tilting maps we have defined. As is standard, this is broken into fifteen terms—four singleton errors, six pair errors, four triple errors, and one quadruple error. We begin with the quadruple error.

Consider
t_{1.S}
, for
S=4
and evaluate the expectation over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.4}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{2n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}\neq\underline{m}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(57)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}\neq\underline{m}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\theta^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\},

where (a) follows from

\displaystyle\theta^{\otimes n}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}},

and (b) follows from

\displaystyle\theta^{\otimes n}=\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),\mbox{ where }\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\theta^{\otimes n}-\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
5
]
. Consider the first term in (
57
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\overline{B}^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\xi^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\Pi\xi^{\otimes n}\Pi\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})2^{-n(H(Y)-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\Pi\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}2^{-n(I(Y;\underline{Z})-2\delta)},

where (a) follows from

\displaystyle\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\leq I-\beta^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\leq I-\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}},

(b) follows from
\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\overline{B}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}^{[4]}
, (c) follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}^{[4]}
in (
32
) and from (ii) the Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
34
, Thm. 3.7]
, (e) follows from (i) the definition of
G^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}
in (
V-A
)
and from (ii) the cyclicity of the trace, and finally (f) and (g) follow from

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\Pi\xi^{\otimes n}\Pi\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(H(Y)-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\Pi\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(H(Y)-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(I(Y;\underline{Z})-2\delta)},\mbox{ for }\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}}).

Now consider the second term in (
57
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m},\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m},\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\norm{ \gamma^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{1}\norm{ \CalN_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right) }_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}\>2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{\max_{S\subseteq[4]}\{|D_{S}|^{n}\}}}.

The first inequality follows from the standard trace-norm inequality

\displaystyle|\tr(AB)|\leq\norm{AB}_{1}\leq\norm{A}_{1}\norm{B}_{\infty}.

(58)

The last inequality follows from (i)

\displaystyle\norm{\gamma^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{1}\leq\norm{I-\beta^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{\infty}\norm{\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y}^{e}}}- \beta^{*}_{\ulinez^{n},\ulined^{n}}\right)}_{1}\leq\norm{I-\beta^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{\infty}^{2}\norm{\Pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }}_{1}\leq 2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n},

and from (ii) the inequality stated in
[
5
]

\displaystyle\norm{ \CalN_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right) }_{\infty}\leq\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{\max_{S\subseteq[4]}\{|D_{S}|^{n}\}}}.

The upper-bound on the
\mathbb{L}_{\infty}
-norm of
\mathcal{N}_{\eta}

shows that the difference between
\theta^{\otimes n}
and
\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)
is small. This establishes the smoothing property. Choosing

\displaystyle\max_{S\subseteq[4]}\{|D_{S}|\}\geq(42\eta^{2})^{\frac{1}{n}}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{2}2^{2(I(Y;\underline{Z})+2\delta)},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.4}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}\left(2^{-n\left(I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right)-2\delta\right)}+2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{\max_{S\subseteq[4]}\{|D_{S}|^{n}\}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\>2^{-n\left(I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right)-2\delta-(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})\right)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mbox{if }\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4}<I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right),\mbox{ then }\mathbb{E}[t_{1.4}]\leq\epsilon.

Returning to the original notation (see Fig.
11
), we have
\mathbb{E}[t_{1.4}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle S_{12}+S_{13}+K_{1}+L_{1}+\max\{S_{21},S_{31}\}<I(Y_{1};U_{12},U_{13},X_{1},U_{21}\oplus U_{31}).

The above bound is obtained when binning is ignored. However, since our analysis includes binning and, moreover, each of the codebooks is picked according to the corresponding marginal distribution, the right-hand side of the bound we obtain will contain an additional divergence term. Specifically, we obtain

\displaystyle S_{12}+S_{13}+K_{1}+L_{1}+\max\{S_{21},S_{31}\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(Y_{1};U_{12},U_{13},X_{1},U_{21}\oplus U_{31})+D\left(p_{U_{12}U_{13}X_{1}}p_{U_{21}\oplus U_{31}}\Big|\Big|\frac{p_{X_{1}}}{|\mathcal{U}_{12}||\mathcal{U}_{13}|\upsilon_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{12}|\right)+\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{13}|\right)+\log\left(\upsilon_{1}\right)+H(X_{1})-H(U_{12},U_{13},X_{1},U_{21}\oplus U_{23}|Y_{1}).

Note that the above bound is identical to the one obtained in (
29
), (
30
)
of the Thm.
2
, with
A_{1}=\{12,13\}
.

Having described the analysis of the quadruple error term, we are left with fourteen terms. The analysis of each of these fourteen terms is essentially identical. Therefore, we describe the analysis for a general
S\subsetneq[4]
.
Consider

\displaystyle t_{1.S}=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{Z^{n}_{S}(\tilde{m}_{S}),D^{n}_{S}(\tilde{m}_{S}),Z^{n}_{S^{c}}(m_{S^{c}}),D^{n}_{S^{c}}(m_{S^{c}})}\theta_{\underline{Z}^{n}(\underline{m}),\underline{D}^{n}(\underline{m})}\right\}.

We evaluate the expectation over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.S}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\theta_{z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\},

where the first equality follows from

\displaystyle\theta_{z^{n}_{S},d^{n}_{S}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{S^{c}}|^{n}}\sum_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\sum_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}|Z_{S}}^{n}(z^{n}_{S^{c}}|z^{n}_{S})\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}},

and the inequality follows

\displaystyle\theta{z^{n}_{S},d^{n}_{S}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\Omega(S,\eta)}{\Omega(\eta)}\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),\mbox{ where }

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}

\displaystyle\theta{z^{n}_{S},d^{n}_{S}}-\frac{\Omega(S,\eta)}{\Omega(\eta)}\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
5
]
. Consider the first term in (
59
)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\Pi_{\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}^{S^{c}}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})}-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}-\overline{B}^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\Pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})2^{-n(H(Y|Z_{S^{c}})-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{-n(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta)}2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)},

where (a) follows from

\displaystyle\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\leq I-\beta^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\leq I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}},

(b) follows from
\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}
having support contained in
\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})
, (c) follows from extracting the isometry
\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}
, (d) follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}^{S}_{\tilde{z}_{S}^{n},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}
in (
32
) and (ii) from Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
34
, Thm. 3.7]
, (e) follows from (i) the definition of
G^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}
in (
V-A
) and from (ii) the cyclicity of the trace, and finally (f) and (g) follow from

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(H(Y|Z_{S^{c}})-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\Pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(H(Y|Z_{S^{c}})-\delta)}\tr\left\{\Pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(I\left(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}}\right)-2\delta\right)},\mbox{ for }(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}}).

Now consider the second term in (
59
). Using the trace-norm inequality (
58
) and the inequality

\displaystyle\norm{\CalN_{d^n_{S^c}}\left(\xi_{z^n_{S^c}} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{\infty}\leq\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}}.

as stated in
[
5
]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\norm{ \gamma^*_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}}_{1}\norm{\CalN_{d^n_{S^c}}\left(\xi_{z^n_{S^c}} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}}2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)}.

Choosing
|\mathcal{D}_{S}|\geq(42\eta)^{\frac{2}{n}}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{2}2^{2\left(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta\right)}
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[t_{1.S}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)}\left(2^{-n(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta)}+2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 22^{-n\left(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta-\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)\right)}

Hence,
\mathbb{E}[t_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}<I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}}).

As an example, we take
S=\{1,2,4\}
, i.e.,
\mathbb{E}[t_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{4}<I(Y;Z_{1},Z_{2},Z_{4}|Z_{3}).

Returning to the original notation (see Fig.
11
), we have
\mathbb{E}[t_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle S_{12}+S_{13}+\max\{S_{21},S_{31}\}<I(Y_{1};U_{12},U_{13},U_{21}\oplus U_{31}|X_{1}).

Now if the analysis is carried out with binning, we obtain the following bound

\displaystyle S_{12}+S_{13}+\max\{S_{21},S_{31}\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(Y_{1};U_{12},U_{13},U_{21}\oplus U_{31}|X_{1})+D\left(p_{U_{12}U_{13}}p_{U_{21}\oplus U_{31}}\Big|\Big|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{12}||\mathcal{U}_{13}|\log(\Prime_1)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{12}|\right)+\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{13}|\right)+\log\left(\upsilon_{1}\right)-H\left(U_{12},U_{13},U_{21}\oplus U_{31}|X_{1},Y_{1}\right),

which corresponds to the bound (
26
), (
27
) in Thm.
2
, with
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\{12,13\}
.
